Let's formulate iterative base B. Let Q length B, sum1, coefficients q_j. Infinite product P∞ has digit distribution Q in base B with infinitely many digits. Then distribution of X has entropy H(Q) per digit; support size B^r. Sum X+Y has max probability. For large r, by central limit (if digit variance finite), sum distribution over 0..2B^r has variance ~r B^2 σ_d^2, max prob ~1/√r, while length B^r -> objective ~B^r/√r huge. Convolution via FFT:
```
def conv(a):
    n=len(a); L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
```
For repeated with same L, maybe use complex? rfft efficient. Need handle tiny negatives due to numerical; we only need max and gradients. Use float64.

Objective and top coefficients:
```
def conv(a, L, fa=None): ...
def objective(a,n): c=conv; M=max(c); return 2*M/n
```

Smooth update in log domain:
```
def mirror_step(a, beta, eta, c=None):
   if c is None: c=conv(a)
   z = c - c.max()
   lp = beta*z
   # if beta large underflow, ok exp(lp - max)
   p = exp(lp - max(lp))
   p /= p.sum()
   # gradient using FFT conv p with a_rev
   g = correlate_via_fft(p,a)
   g = 2*g
   # step in loga: loga -= eta * g / max(g)
   loga = np.log(a + eps)
   loga = loga - eta * (g / gmax)
   # rescale to sum n: add log(n/sum(exp(loga)))
   m=np.max(loga); e=np.exp(loga-m); a2=e*(n/e.sum())
   return a2
```
Potential issue: loga has unbounded; rescale. Use stable. If some a very small, log negative large; fine. eta maybe 0.05-0.2. Accept based on exact/smooth. Actually if c ~n, want b*c differences O(1) for medium smoothing. If b = B/n, then b*c ~B. Start B=1, anneal to B=20 maybe. For hard, B=100. Use `logb = np.log(b)`. In smooth run, schedule B from 1 to 50.

Parametric Fourier optimization idea:
- Choose base a0 (left-geom or parent) normalized.
- Define a(theta)_i = a0_i * exp(∑_{j=1}^{m} theta_j cos(2π j i/n) + ∑ theta_{m+j} sin(...)). Center modes frequencies maybe 1..50.
- Smooth objective logsumexp with high beta. Gradient w.r.t a plus chain. Use L-BFGS or Adam. This can explore global smooth changes. Since dimension 100, FFT objective/gradient per function eval. We can do thousands. This might find a better shape than coordinate local.
- We can also optimize a smooth addition: `log a_i = log a0_i + ∑ theta_j B_j(i)`, where B_j are low-frequency basis (e.g., Chebyshev or Fourier). Ensure nonnegative by normalization and clipping (exp preserves positive). Use small m (e.g., 60). Then refine full dimension.

Can we implement L-BFGS with numpy? `scipy.optimize.minimize` method 'L-BFGS-B' can use finite diff or user gradient. User gradient lower-dimensional. It may evaluate 100 variables, each objective uses FFT conv + gradient FFT? We need gradient w.r.t theta: For each eval, compute c, p, ga (n-vector), then grad_theta_r = ∑_i ga_i a_i * ∂u_i/∂theta_r (since ∂log a_i = ∂u_i), if a = base*exp(u)/sum. This is matvec n×m. m=100, n=4096 => 400k per eval, okay. scipy L-BFGS may call 1000 evals -> 400M ops, maybe heavy but okay in 2400? Need optimize. We can implement our own Adam/line search for theta.

But target 1.502 requires both global shape and local coordinate. We can combine:
1. Generate left-geom a_base.
2. Run full-dim smooth/hard refine (fast) to local optimum maybe ~1.57? Actually full coordinate from left may maybe improve to ~1.52 if enough.
3. Run low-dim Fourier optimization from there to adjust shape over 100 modes, using smooth objective with high beta. This can move better than gradient in full dimensions? It might escape full-dimensional local minima.

def hard_polish(a, n, deadline, eta0=0.05, tag='hard', K=1, beta=None):
  c=conv(a); best...
  eta=eta0
  momentum=0.0 initially; maybe v=zeros
  it=0
  while time<deadline:
     if it%... print
     # choose active weights
     if K==1: k=argmax(c); p_full zeros; loop g
     else: choose top K by argpartition(c,K); weights = exp((c[top]-cmax)/temp)
     g = active_gradient(a, top, weights)  # length n
     g -= g.mean()? For normalized projection, subtract mean to preserve sum direction? If we project by normalize, not necessary but helps. Subtract mean of g over all.
     mg=max(abs(g)); step=eta* g/mg
     # momentum: v = 0.9*v + step? maybe not.
     trial=normalize_sum(a - step)
     c2=conv(trial); M2=max(c2)
     obj2=2*M2/n
     if obj2 < obj - 1e-12: accept; if improved repeatedly increase eta up to 0.2; decrease if not too often.
     else trial= a? keep, eta*=0.7
     c=c2 if accept else c
```

Let's define a general mirror optimization loop:
```
def mirror_active_opt(a, n, deadline, width=0.05, eta=0.1, temp_frac=0.1, tag):
  c=conv(a)
  best...
  while time:
     M=max(c); thresh=M - width*n  # width in sum=n units; M~0.7n, width 0.05 =>0.037n
     idx=np.where(c>=thresh)[0]; if len(idx)>MAX: idx=top MAX by argsort
     w=softmax((c[idx]-M)/temp)  # ensures sum1
     p=zeros(2n-1); p[idx]=w
     if active_count < 100: d=direct_active_grad(a, idx, 2*w)
     else: d=grad_fft(p,a) *2? (grad_fft returns ∑ p_k a_{k-i}; multiply 2)
     scale=max(abs(d)); if small break
     # mirror step
     loga = np.log(a+1e-300)
     loga -= eta * d / scale
     # renormalize to sum n
     m=max(loga); expa=np.exp(loga-m); a2=expa*(n/expa.sum())
     c2=conv(a2); M2=max(c2); obj2=2*M2/n
     accept if obj2<best or with annealing
     adapt eta
```
The scale `max(d)` maybe ~O(max a); eta maybe 0.05-0.5. We can do backtracking with a few candidate etas? To save cost, one step. Use adaptive: if improve, eta = min(eta*1.05, 0.5); else eta=max(eta*0.7,0.01). Also if many fails, reset.

Use annealing: initially eta moderate. For broad active, step may be smooth.

Hard direct gradient for active weights:
```
def active_gradient_direct(a, idx, w, factor=2.0):
   n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
   for k, wk in zip(idx,w):
      if wk ==0: continue
      js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)+1
      # g[js:je] = factor*wk * a[k - arange(js,je)]
      g[js:je] += factor*wk * a[k - np.arange(js,je)]
   return g
```

FFT gradient function for dense p length 2n-1:
```
def gradient_conv_weights(a,p,Lg=None):
    # returns g = sum_m a_m p[i+m]
    Lg = nextpow2(3*n-2)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    f1=np.fft.rfft(p,Lg)
    f2=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,Lg)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(f1*f2,Lg)
    g=conv[n-1:n-1+n]
    return g
```

Active indices selection:
```
def _select_active(c, K, random_frac=0.0, rng=None):
    nlen=len(c)
    if K >= nlen:
       return np.arange(nlen)
    # We can choose top K by partition; then optionally random subset among top? 
    top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    # sort descending optional for weights
    top = top[np.argsort(c[top])[::-1]]
    return top
```
For random broad, if K small and random active, maybe choose K random from mask c>M-slack. Let's implement mode:
- mode 'top': return topK.
- mode 'rand': threshold = M - slack_width*n; candidates = where(c>=threshold); if len candidates <K, topK; else choose K random.

Weights:
```
def _active_weights(c_active, M, n, K):
   minv=c_active.min()
   spread = M - minv
   tau = max(n*1e-6, 0.05*spread + n*1e-8) # too broad maybe; maybe 0.2*spread
   w=np.exp((c_active-M)/tau)
   w /= w.sum()
```
If K top and spread small, uniform. If K top includes much lower, weights favor high.
Could use `tau= (M - minv)/K` to make decay over K. `tau = max(1e-6, (M - minv)/3.0)` maybe.

Direct gradient:
```
def _grad_direct(a, idx, w, factor=2.0):
    n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
    for kk, ww in zip(idx,w):
       if ww <= 0: continue
       js = kk - (n-1)
       if js < 0: js = 0
       je = kk + 1
       if je > n: je = n
       if js < je:
           # indices of a are kk - js ... kk - (je-1)
           g[js:je] += factor * ww * a[kk - np.arange(js,je)]
    return g
```
We can precompute `np.arange(js,je)` each loop. For speed, if many, maybe use slicing with reversed views: `a[kk-je+1:kk-js+1][::-1]`. Better:
```

def _mirror_trial(loga, d, scale, eta, n):

   # to avoid underflow, shift

Let's detail `mirror_run`:
```
def _mirror_run(a, n, deadline, tag, k_start=64, k_end=1, eta0=0.22, use_random=False, max_iter=None):
    a = normalize_sum(a,n)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    c = _conv_self(a)
    M = float(c.max()); obj=2*M/n; best=a.copy(); best_obj=obj
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=max(1.0, deadline-t0)
    eta=eta0; fails=0; it=0; last_print=t0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(hash(tag)%2**31) # deterministic? Use global? We can pass seed.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       rem=max(0.0,(deadline-time.monotonic())/total)
       K = int(k_start * rem*rem + k_end)
       K = max(k_end, min(k_start, K))
       M = float(c.max()); obj=2*M/n
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
          best_obj=obj; best=a.copy()
       # active selection
       if use_random and K <= 4:
          # random among top threshold
          thresh = M - max(1e-5*n, 0.005*n)
          cand = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
          if cand.size ==0: cand=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
          if cand.size > 200:
              idx = rng.choice(cand, size=K, replace=False)
          else: idx=cand[:K] maybe if K>size; if K< size and want K random
       else:
          if K >= len(c): idx=np.arange(len(c))
          else:
              idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       # weights
       ca = c[idx]
       Mca = M
       spread = Mca - float(ca.min())
       tau = max(1e-6*n, 0.25*spread)
       # if K=1 tau irrelevant
       if K >1:
          w = np.exp((ca - Mca)/tau)
          w /= w.sum()
       else: w=np.array([1.0])
       d = _grad_direct(a, idx, w)
       dmean = float(d.mean())
       d = d - dmean
       scale = float(d.max() - d.min())
       if scale < 1e-14:
          # perturb loga slightly
          loga += (rng.normal(0,1e-6,a.size)); a=...; continue
       ds = d / scale
       # try steps
       improved=False
       # adaptive temperature for accepting slightly worse
       T = max(1e-6, 2e-5*rem)
       for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           e = eta*mult
           a2=loga2 = _mirror_trial(loga, ds, e, n)
           if a2 is None: break
           c2 = _conv_self(a2); M2=float(c2.max()); obj2=2*M2/n
           if obj2 < obj + T * rng.random():
               loga = loga2; a=a2; c=c2; M=M2; obj=obj2
               improved=True
               if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
                   best_obj=obj2; best=a.copy()
               # adapt eta
               fails=0
               if obj2 < obj_old - 1e-10:
                   eta = min(eta*1.07, 0.9)
               break
       if not improved:
           eta = max(eta*0.7, 0.02)
           fails +=1
           if fails > 20:
               # reset by small perturbation
               loga = loga + rng.normal(0, 1e-4, a.size)
               a = _loga_to_a(loga,n)
               c = _conv_self(a)
               obj = 2*float(c.max())/n; fails=0; eta=0.15
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic()-last_print > 15:
          print(f"[{tag}] obj={best_obj:.6f} cur={obj:.6f} K={K} eta={eta:.3f} it={it}")
          last_print=time.monotonic()
    # final maybe take best with current c? return best_obj
    return best, best_obj, loga maybe
```

def _select_active(c, K, rng, tie_tol=1e-5*n):
    M=c.max()
    tol = max(1e-12*n, tie_tol*n?) # objective units; for n=3809, 1e-5*3809=0.038 maybe small; tied floats may differ 1e-12.
    cand = np.where(c >= M - tie_tol)[0]

        else: idx=cand
    # ensure top high values: if cand based tie maybe includes not top? We can sort by c? For nearly max okay.

```
def _select_active(c, K, rng, tol_frac=0.002):
    M = float(c.max())
    tol = max(1e-12, tol_frac * M)
    cand = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - tol)
    if cand.size == 0:
        cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    if cand.size <= K:
        return cand
    # If K>=10, choose evenly across cand to cover plateau
    if K >= 8:
        # If top set huge, maybe choose first K? evenly better
        pos = np.linspace(0, cand.size - 1, K).astype(np.int64)
        idx = cand[pos]
    else:
        idx = rng.choice(cand, size=K, replace=False)
    # Ensure we always include the true max? cand includes; even pos may not. Add argmax.
    kmax = int(np.argmax(c))
    if kmax not in idx: maybe replace lowest weight? Simpler: if kmax not in idx, idx[-1]=kmax? But weights include near max; not critical. Could do `idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx, np.array([kmax]))))[:K]?` Might break length. We'll just if K==1 return argmax; else if kmax not in idx, set idx[0]=kmax.
    return idx
```
`np.flatnonzero` over 7617 each iter O(n). Fine.
Tie_tol 0.002M. For M ~3000, tol=6. This includes coefficients within 0.2% of max, maybe many. If cand huge, evenly choose K. Good. As optimization narrows, active set fewer.

Weights:
```
def _weights_active(c, idx, M):
    if len(idx)==1: return 1,
    ca=c[idx]; mn=ca.min(); mx=M
    spread=mx-mn
    tau=max(1e-12, 0.5*spread + 1e-12)
    w=np.exp((ca-mx)/tau)
    # avoid underflow
    if not finite or sum0: uniform
    else w/=w.sum()
```
If cand wide with spread maybe >0, weights favor max. Good.

Direct gradient with 64 idx and vector slices. Use local variables for speed.
```
def _grad_direct(a, idx, w):
    n=a.size; g=np.empty(n); g.fill(0.0)
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
       k=int(idx[ii]); ww=float(w[ii])
       if ww ==0: continue
       s = k - n + 1
       if s < 0: s = 0
       e = k + 1
       if e > n: e = n
       if s < e:
           # source slice: a[k-e+1 : k-s+1] Reversed
           g[s:e] += (2.0*ww) * a[k-e+1:k-s+1][::-1]
    return g
```
Test k=0,n=3: s=0,e=1, source a[0:1][::-1]=a0, g0=2ww*a0. correct. k=1: s=0,e=2, source a[0:2][::-1]=[a1,a0], g0=2a1,g1=2a0. c1 derivative wrt a0=2a1, a1=2a0. correct.

For weights sum1, factor2; d range maybe ~2*max. Scaling cancels.

`_mirror_trial`: Need ds may contain large values if scale small? We normalized scale=max-min. If scale<1e-12 return None.
```
def _mirror_trial(loga, ds, eta, n):
    val = loga - eta * ds
    m = float(np.max(val))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return None,None
    shifted = val - m
    # To avoid loga2n with -inf causing issues next, clip lower -745
    shifted_clip = np.where(shifted < -745.0, -745.0, shifted)
    e = np.exp(shifted_clip)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <=0 or not finite: return None,None
    a = e * (n/s)
    loga2 = val - m + np.log(n/s)
    loga2[~np.isfinite(loga2)] = -745.0
    return a, loga2
```
If some val very negative, shifted_clip -745, exp ~5e-324; sum ok. a may have zeros due to underflow but loga2 -745. Good.

```
noise=np.zeros(n)
ii=np.arange(n)
for r in range(modes):
   freq=int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n//100))) # low up to 40
   phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   amp=float(rng.normal(0,1.0))
   noise += amp * np.cos((2*np.pi*freq*ii)/n + phase)
noise -= noise.mean()
std=np.sqrt(np.mean(noise*noise))
if std>1e-12: noise/=std
a2=np.exp(np.log(a+1e-300)+sigma*noise)
return normalize(a2,n)
```

_N_CUR = 0
_L_SELF = 0
def _prepare_fft(n):
  global _N_CUR, _L_SELF
  _N_CUR=n
  L=1
  while L < 2*n -1: L <<=1
  _L_SELF=L
```

Let's code `_mirror_run` robustly:
```
def _mirror_run(a, n, deadline, tag, seed, k_start=128, k_end=1, eta_start=0.25, tol_start=0.02, tol_end=0.002, verbose=True):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    c = _conv_self(a)
    M = float(c.max()); obj = 2.0*M/n
    best = a.copy(); best_obj = obj
    t0 = time.monotonic(); total = max(1e-6, deadline - t0)
    eta=eta_start; fails=0; it=0; last_print=t0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       rem = (deadline - time.monotonic()) / total
       rem = max(0.0, min(1.0, rem))
       K = int(round(k_start * rem * rem + k_end))
       if K < k_end: K=k_end
       if K > 2*n-1: K=2*n-1
       tol_frac = tol_end + (tol_start - tol_end)*rem
       M = float(c.max()); obj=2.0*M/n
       if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
           best_obj=obj; best=a.copy()
       idx = _select_active(c, K, rng, tol_frac)
       w = _weights_active(c, idx, M)
       d = _grad_direct(a, idx, w)
       dmean = float(d.mean())
       d = d - dmean
       scale = float(d.max() - d.min())
       if scale < 1e-14:
           # perturb
           loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) + rng.normal(0,1e-4,a.size)
           a = _loga_to_a(loga, n); c = _conv_self(a); obj=2*float(c.max())/n; continues? continue
       ds = d / scale
       T = max(1e-7, 5e-5 * rem)
       improved=False
       obj_cur = obj
       for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
           e = eta * mult
           a2, loga2 = _mirror_trial(loga, ds, e, n)
           if a2 is None: break
           c2 = _conv_self(a2)
           M2 = float(c2.max()); obj2 = 2.0*M2/n
           if obj2 < obj_cur + T * rng.random():
               loga = loga2; a = a2; c = c2; obj = obj2
               improved=True
               if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
                   best_obj = obj2; best = a.copy()
               if obj2 <= obj_cur - 1e-10:
                   eta = min(eta*1.05, 0.9)
               else:
                   eta = min(eta*1.0, 0.9)
               fails=0
               break
       if not improved:
           eta = max(eta*0.75, 0.02)
           fails +=1
           if fails > 50:
               # mild global perturbation
               loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
               loga += rng.normal(0, 0.02, a.size)
               a = _loga_to_a(loga,n)
               c = _conv_self(a)
               obj = 2*float(c.max())/n
               eta = max(eta, 0.15)
               fails = 0
       it +=1
       if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
           print(f"[{tag}] best={best_obj:.6f} cur={obj:.6f} K={K} eta={eta:.3f} it={it}")
           last_print = time.monotonic()
    return best, best_obj
```
We update best after trial directly. Before trial, M used for weights from c. Good.

In `scale` check, if fails, do we need `continue` after perturbation? We can just set and continue to loop top. Use `continue` to avoid trial with old ds. In code above, if scale small, after perturb `continue`. Good.

def _weights_active(c, idx, M):
   m=idx.size
   if m==1: return np.array([1.0])
   ca=c[idx]
   mn=float(ca.min()); mx=float(M)
   spread=mx-mn
   tau=max(1e-20, 0.5*spread + 1e-12*max(1.0,n?))
```

```
def _make_valid(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.size != n: # resize? fallback
      a=np.ones(n)
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
       return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
   a = a / s * n
   mx=float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
   if mx > 1000.0:
       a = a * (1000.0/mx)
   # if sum too small after cap scale? It's scale invariant; if s after scaling <0.01? n large; maybe if n=1 and mx huge -> scale to 1? sum=min? If n=1, sum=1000? okay.
   if float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
       return np.ones(n)
   return a
```

Let's code `_euclid_run` carefully:
```
def _euclid_run(a,n,deadline,tag,seed,K=16,eta_start=0.10):
   a=_normalize(a,n); c=_conv_self(a); best=...; t0...
   while time<deadline:
      rem...
      M=...; obj=...
      if obj<best...
      idx=_select_active(c,K,rng,0.005)
      w=_weights_active(...)
      d=_grad_direct(a,idx,w)
      d=d-d.mean(); scale=max(abs(d)); if scale<... break
      ds=d/scale
      improved=False
      for mult ...
         trial = a - e*ds
         trial = np.maximum(trial,0)
         s=trial.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         trial *= n/s
         c2=conv; obj2...
         if obj2 < obj + T*rng.random(): accept
      if not improved: eta*=0.75; fails++
      if fails>30:
         a=np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0,0.02*a.size**? ) )
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative height sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
while respecting the evaluator's positivity / clipping behavior.

The method is a hybrid of:
  - analytic Cholesky / geometric starts,
  - exponentiated-gradient (mirror) descent on sampled near-active
    convolution coefficients,
  - projected Euclidean active-set descent,
  - low-frequency and block perturbations for restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_N_CUR = 0
_L_SELF = 0
_LOG_FLOOR = -600.0


def _prepare_fft(n):
    global _N_CUR, _L_SELF
    _N_CUR = int(n)
    L = 1
    need = 2 * _N_CUR - 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    _L_SELF = L


def _normalize(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(n) / s)


def _conv_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n != _N_CUR:
        _prepare_fft(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, _L_SELF)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, _L_SELF)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def _obj_n(a, n):
    c = _conv_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = max(0.0, M) if np.isfinite(M) else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * M / float(n))


def _left_geo(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2), truncated to length n, sum-scaled to n."""
    g = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return g
    g[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        g[i + 1] = g[i] * ((2 * i + 1) / (2.0 * (i + 1)))
    return _normalize(g, n)


def _parent_to_n(raw, n):
    p = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == n:
        a = p.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        pp = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
        lp = np.log(pp)
        ln = np.interp(x_new, x_old, lp)
        a = np.exp(ln - float(np.max(ln)))
    return _normalize(a, n)


def _loga_to_a(loga, n):
    v = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), v
    shifted = np.where(v - m < _LOG_FLOOR, _LOG_FLOOR, v - m)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), v
    a = e * (float(n) / s)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(v - m + np.log(float(n) / s)),
                   v - m + np.log(float(n) / s),
                   _LOG_FLOOR)
    return a, out


def _mirror_trial(loga, ds, eta, n):
    v = loga - eta * ds
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return None, None
    shifted = np.where(v - m < _LOG_FLOOR, _LOG_FLOOR, v - m)
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None, None
    a = e * (float(n) / s)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(v - m + np.log(float(n) / s)),
                   v - m + np.log(float(n) / s),
                   _LOG_FLOOR)
    return a, out


def _select_active(c, K, rng, tol_frac):
    L = len(c)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    K = max(1, int(K))
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L, dtype=int)

    M = float(c.max())
    tol = max(1e-14, float(tol_frac) * max(1.0, M))
    cand = np.flatnonzero(c >= M - tol)
    kmax = int(np.argmax(c))

    if cand.size == 0:
        return np.array([kmax], dtype=int)
    if cand.size <= K:
        return cand.astype(int, copy=False)

    if K == 1:
        return np.array([kmax], dtype=int)

    # Evenly sample the near-active plateau to avoid index bias.
    pos = (np.arange(K) * cand.size // K).astype(np.int64)
    idx = cand[pos].astype(int, copy=False)

    if kmax not in idx:
        vals = c[idx]
        minpos = int(np.argmin(vals))
        idx[minpos] = kmax
    return idx


def _weights_active(c, idx, M):
    m = idx.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    ca = c[idx].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    mn = float(ca.min())
    spread = max(0.0, float(M) - mn)
    tau = max(1e-12, 0.5 * spread)
    w = np.exp((ca - float(M)) / tau)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / s
    return w


def _grad_direct(a, idx, w):
    """
    Gradient factors for sampled convolution coefficients.
    For coefficient k, d/d a_i of C_k is 2 a_{k-i} when valid.
    """
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
        k = int(idx[ii])
        ww = float(w[ii])
        if ww == 0.0:
            continue
        s = k - n + 1
        if s < 0:
            s = 0
        e = k + 1
        if e > n:
            e = n
        if s < e:
            # a[k-e+1 : k-s+1] reversed gives a[k-i] for i=s..e-1.
            g[s:e] += (2.0 * ww) * a[k - e + 1:k - s + 1][::-1]
    return g


def _mirror_run(a, n, deadline, tag, seed, k_start=128, k_end=1,
                eta_start=0.25, tol_start=0.02, tol_end=0.002,
                verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    loga_float = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga, _ = _loga_to_a(loga_float, n)
    c = _conv_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    obj = float(2.0 * M / n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1e-6, deadline - t0)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    fails = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = (deadline - time.monotonic()) / total
        rem = min(1.0, max(0.0, rem))

        K = int(round(k_start * rem * rem + k_end))
        K = max(k_end, min(k_start, K, 2 * n - 1))
        tol_frac = tol_end + (tol_start - tol_end) * rem

        M = float(c.max())
        obj = float(2.0 * M / n)
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = _select_active(c, K, rng, tol_frac)
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)

        w = _weights_active(c, idx, M)
        d = _grad_direct(a, idx, w)
        d = d - float(d.mean())
        scale = float(d.max() - d.min())

        if scale < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(scale):
            loga_float = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga_float += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n)
            a, loga = _loga_to_a(loga_float, n)
            c = _conv_self(a)
            continue

        ds = d / scale
        T = max(1e-7, 5e-5 * rem)
        cur_obj = obj
        accepted = False

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            e = eta * mult
            a2, loga2 = _mirror_trial(loga, ds, e, n)
            if a2 is None:
                continue
            c2 = _conv_self(a2)
            M2 = float(c2.max())
            obj2 = float(2.0 * M2 / n)
            if not np.isfinite(obj2):
                continue
            if obj2 < cur_obj + T * rng.random():
                loga = loga2
                a = a2
                c = c2
                obj = obj2
                if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
                    best_obj = obj2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                if obj2 <= cur_obj - 1e-10:
                    eta = min(eta * 1.05, 0.90)
                else:
                    eta = min(eta, 0.90)
                fails = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta = max(eta * 0.75, 0.02)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 50:
                loga_float = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                loga_float += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n)
                a, loga = _loga_to_a(loga_float, n)
                c = _conv_self(a)
                obj = float(2.0 * float(c.max()) / n)
                eta = max(eta, 0.15)
                fails = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] best={best_obj:.6f} cur={obj:.6f} "
                  f"K={K} eta={eta:.3f} it={it}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a, best_obj


def _euclid_run(a, n, deadline, tag, seed, K=18, eta_start=0.10, verbose=False):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    c = _conv_self(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    obj = float(2.0 * M / n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1e-6, deadline - t0)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    fails = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = (deadline - time.monotonic()) / total
        rem = min(1.0, max(0.0, rem))
        M = float(c.max())
        obj = float(2.0 * M / n)
        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best_a = a.copy()

        idx = _select_active(c, K, rng, 0.005)
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        w = _weights_active(c, idx, M)
        d = _grad_direct(a, idx, w)
        d = d - float(d.mean())
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(scale):
            break
        ds = d / scale
        T = max(1e-7, 1e-5 * rem)
        accepted = False

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            e = eta * mult
            trial = a - e * ds
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            s = float(trial.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            trial *= (float(n) / s)
            c2 = _conv_self(trial)
            M2 = float(c2.max())
            obj2 = float(2.0 * M2 / n)
            if not np.isfinite(obj2):
                continue
            if obj2 < obj + T * rng.random():
                a = trial
                c = c2
                obj = obj2
                if obj2 < best_obj - 1e-12:
                    best_obj = obj2
                    best_a = a.copy()
                if obj2 <= obj - 1e-10:
                    eta = min(eta * 1.05, 0.35)
                fails = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta = max(eta * 0.75, 0.02)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 40:
                a = np.maximum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n), 0.0)
                a = _normalize(a, n)
                c = _conv_self(a)
                obj = float(2.0 * float(c.max()) / n)
                eta = max(eta, 0.08)
                fails = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 20.0:
            print(f"[{tag}] euclid best={best_obj:.6f} cur={obj:.6f} "
                  f"eta={eta:.3f} it={it}")
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a, best_obj


def _mix_log(a, b, lam):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), len(a))
    b = _normalize(np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64), len(b))
    n = a.size
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    lb = np.log(np.maximum(b, 1e-300))
    mix = float(lam) * la + (1.0 - float(lam)) * lb
    return _normalize(np.exp(mix - float(np.max(mix))), n)


def _perturb_block(a, n, rng, sigma, width_frac):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    w = max(2, int(n * max(0.005, width_frac)))
    w = min(w, n)
    s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
    fac = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=w))
    b = a.copy()
    b[s:s + w] *= fac
    return _normalize(b, n)


def _perturb_fourier(a, n, rng, sigma, n_modes=8):
    a = _normalize(a, n)
    ii = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    maxfreq = max(2, n // 80)
    for _ in range(max(1, int(n_modes))):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, maxfreq + 1))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
        noise += amp * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * freq * ii) / float(n) + phase)
    noise -= float(noise.mean())
    std = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(noise * noise)))
    if std > 1e-14:
        noise /= std
    b = np.exp(np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)) + float(sigma) * noise)
    return _normalize(b, n)


def _make_valid(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = (a / s) * float(n)
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if mx > 1000.0 and mx > 0.0:
        a *= (1000.0 / mx)
    if float(a.sum()) < 0.01:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def propose_candidate():
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    HARD_DEADLINE = t0 + 2400.0 - 40.0

    raw_parent = None
    try:
        raw_parent = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
    except Exception:
        raw_parent = None

    parent = None
    if raw_parent is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and 500 <= p.size <= 15000:
                n = int(p.size)
                parent = _parent_to_n(p, n)
            else:
                n = 3809
        except Exception:
            n = 3809
    else:
        n = 3809
        parent = _parent_to_n(raw_parent, n)

    if n < 50:
        n = 3809

    _prepare_fft(n)

    left_a = _left_geo(n)
    if parent is not None:
        parent = _normalize(parent, n)

    rng_master = np.random.default_rng(20240566)

    seeds = []
    if parent is not None:
        po = _obj_n(parent, n)
        seeds.append((po, parent, "parent"))
    lo = _obj_n(left_a, n)
    seeds.append((lo, left_a, "left"))
    if parent is not None:
        blend = _mix_log(parent, left_a, 0.5)
        bo = _obj_n(blend, n)
        seeds.append((bo, blend, "blend"))

    best_a = None
    best_obj = np.inf
    for o, v, name in seeds:
        if np.isfinite(o) and o < best_obj:
            best_obj = o
            best_a = v.copy()
    if best_a is None:
        best_obj = lo
        best_a = left_a.copy()

    print(f"n={n} initial best_obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Two brief exploratory refinements: one from the best seed, one from a
    # low-frequency perturbation to escape any local minimum of the seed.
    if time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 180:
        q1_end = min(time.monotonic() + 70.0, HARD_DEADLINE)
        a1, o1 = _mirror_run(
            best_a, n, q1_end, "quick0", 101,
            k_start=96, k_end=1, eta_start=0.28,
            tol_start=0.02, tol_end=0.002, verbose=False
        )
        if o1 < best_obj:
            best_obj, best_a = o1, a1

    pert0 = _perturb_fourier(best_a, n, rng_master, 0.12, 10)
    if time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 120:
        q2_end = min(time.monotonic() + 70.0, HARD_DEADLINE)
        a2, o2 = _mirror_run(
            pert0, n, q2_end, "quick1", 102,
            k_start=96, k_end=1, eta_start=0.28,
            tol_start=0.02, tol_end=0.002, verbose=False
        )
        if o2 < best_obj:
            best_obj, best_a = o2, a2

    # Main long refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 820:
        main_end = min(time.monotonic() + 900.0, HARD_DEADLINE - 780.0)
        if main_end > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            best_a, best_obj = _mirror_run(
                best_a, n, main_end, "main", 103,
                k_start=128, k_end=1, eta_start=0.28,
                tol_start=0.02, tol_end=0.002, verbose=True
            )
        print(f"after main: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # A shorter Euclidean active-set polish; useful when mirror dynamics stall.
    if time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 650:
        eu_end = min(time.monotonic() + 100.0, HARD_DEADLINE - 600.0)
        if eu_end > time.monotonic() + 20.0:
            best_a, best_obj = _euclid_run(
                best_a, n, eu_end, "euclid", 104,
                K=20, eta_start=0.10, verbose=False
            )
        print(f"after euclid: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Restart loop: perturb in different ways, then polish briefly.
    sources = [best_a.copy(), left_a.copy()]
    if parent is not None:
        sources.append(parent.copy())

    cycle = 0
    while time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 130:
        mode = cycle % 4
        sigma = 0.08 + 0.55 * min(1.0, float(cycle) / 50.0)
        sigma = min(sigma, 0.85)

        if mode == 0:
            wf = float(rng_master.choice([0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20]))
            cand = _perturb_block(best_a, n, rng_master, sigma, wf)
        elif mode == 1:
            cand = _perturb_fourier(best_a, n, rng_master, sigma,
                                    n_modes=int(6 + (cycle % 10)))
        elif mode == 2:
            src = sources[(cycle // 4) % len(sources)]
            lam = float(rng_master.uniform(0.20, 0.70))
            cand = _mix_log(best_a, src, lam)
        else:
            lam = float(rng_master.uniform(0.30, 0.75))
            cand = _mix_log(best_a, left_a, lam)

        end = min(time.monotonic() + 28.0, HARD_DEADLINE - 130.0)
        if end <= time.monotonic() + 5.0:
            break
        aq, oq = _mirror_run(
            cand, n, end, f"r{cycle}", 20000 + cycle,
            k_start=80, k_end=1, eta_start=0.25,
            tol_start=0.015, tol_end=0.002, verbose=False
        )
        if oq < best_obj:
            best_obj, best_a = oq, aq
            print(f"restart {cycle}: new best={best_obj:.6f}")
        cycle += 1

    # Final polish on the champion.
    if time.monotonic() < HARD_DEADLINE - 5:
        final_end = HARD_DEADLINE
        best_a, best_obj = _mirror_run(
            best_a, n, final_end, "final", 777,
            k_start=48, k_end=1, eta_start=0.22,
            tol_start=0.012, tol_end=0.002, verbose=False
        )
        print(f"after final: obj={best_obj:.6f}")

    # Choose the best valid sequence by the actual evaluator when possible.
    trial_lists = []
    seen = set()

    def _add_candidate(arr):
        key = None
        if arr is None:
            return
        try:
            key = float(np.mean(arr[:min(16, len(arr))]))
            key = (len(arr), key)
        except Exception:
            return
        if key in seen:
            return
        seen.add(key)
        valid = _make_valid(arr, n)
        trial_lists.append([float(x) for x in valid])

    _add_candidate(best_a)
    _add_candidate(parent)
    _add_candidate(left_a)
    _add_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if not trial_lists:
        trial_lists.append([1.0] * n)

    best_val = np.inf
    best_list = trial_lists[0]
    for lst in trial_lists:
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_list = lst

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val > 2.1:
        best_list = [1.0] * n

    print(f"FINAL objective_internal={best_obj:.6f} final_sequence_len={n}")
    return best_list
```
